Each premise/conclusion line: fol, nl = line.split(':::', 1). Keep the story id (entry id) and the declared predicate list (name, arity, gloss) for B7.\n1.3 Build the gold sentence list from folio-validation.jsonl: zip premises with premises-FOL, plus conclusion with conclusion-FOL. Handle both list and newline-joined string fields.\n1.4 norm(s) = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', s.lower()).strip().rstrip('.'). Match Logic-LM NL lines to gold NL by norm equality. Log n_lines, n_matched and n_unmatched per system; unmatched lines are excluded.\n1.5 Gold per sentence: corrected if available (gold_source='corrected'), else original. Parse and z3-check satisfiability. Exclude unparseable or unsat golds and log the counts with examples.\n1.6 unique matched sentences → sort by sha1(norm(s)).hexdigest() → first 300 (all of them if fewer, and log that). Real candidates = every (sentence, system) pair where that system has a line for it, about 900. item_id = f'{sha1[:10]}:{system}'. Keep a duplicate NL in several stories only once, using the first story in sorted id order for context.\n1.7 fol_parse.py:\n  - Extend the prototype grammar to cover: ∀x ∃x (and 'all x'/'exists x' tolerated? NO — only the symbols above); multiple-letter variables; constants with digits and underscores; = and ≠; zero-arity atoms; ⊕ and ↔.\n  - Anything else (∈, ⊆, '::', 'ForAll(', LaTeX) raises ParseError, so parse_ok=False.\n  - Free variables: universally close them and set flag free_vars=True.\n  - Store an AST dataclass.\n1.8 labeler.py: label(cand_ast, gold_ast) -> {status ∈ equiv/nonequiv/unlabeled, bounded_equiv, unbounded_status, bijection, seconds}.\n  - If the arity multiset of predicates differs, or the constant counts differ → nonequiv, without solver work. Constants may map to constants.\n  - Enumerate arity-preserving bijections (product of per-arity permutations and constant permutations). If the count exceeds 5040, or there are more than 8 predicates → unlabeled.\n  - Fast reject: pre-sample 64 random interpretations over domain sizes 2 and 3 using a pure-Python evaluator on the AST; drop a bijection if any sample gives different truth values.\n  - Surviving bijections: z3 bounded check ¬(Fc[σ] ↔ Fg) is UNSAT on each EnumSort size 1..4. Use a UNIQUE sort name per call (global counter).\n  - Equiv iff some σ passes all sizes. For equiv items also run an unbounded z3 check (uninterpreted sort, 10 s timeout) and log {unsat, sat, unknown}.\n  - Overall per-candidate timeout of 30 s (enforce via ProcessPoolExecutor future.result(timeout) and a fresh worker) → unlabeled.\n  - Label correct=1 iff equiv. Unparseable candidates get correct=0 (they are wrong outputs) but are flagged.\n  - If corrected gold exists, label under BOTH golds → correct_orig and correct_corr, and use correct_corr as primary.\n  - The labeler NEVER reads names.\n1.9 mutate.py (seed=0; random.Random(sha1-derived seed per sentence)). One mutant per operator per gold, from:\n  - NEG: remove a random ¬ if any, else wrap a random atom in ¬.\n  - QUANT: ∀x(A→B) ↦ ∃x(A∧B) and ∃x(A∧B) ↦ ∀x(A→B); else flip the outermost quantifier; N/A if no quantifier.\n  - IMPL_REV: swap the sides of a random →.\n  - DROP: remove one conjunct of a random ∧ with ≥2 conjuncts, preferring antecedents.\n  - ADD: conjoin Q(v) to the antecedent (or to the matrix if there is no →), where Q is a unary predicate from another gold formula of the same story not in F (seeded), else a fresh 'Extra' predicate.\n  - ARG_SWAP: R(a,b) ↦ R(b,a) on a random binary atom.\n  - ANDOR: flip a random ∧/∨.\n  - SCOPE: prenex, then swap two adjacent quantifiers of different type; N/A if none.\n  - MERGE: replace all occurrences of unary Q by unary P (P≠Q); N/A if fewer than 2 unary predicates.\n  - CARD: replace a subformula ∃x φ with ∃x∃y(x≠y ∧ φ(x) ∧ φ(y)); for ground or ∀-only formulas, N/A.\n  Keep a mutant only if the labeler's identity-bijection check says NON-equivalent over sizes 1..4. Log n_generated, n_equiv_dropped and n_NA per operator.\n  Rewrites: 2 per gold, drawn by seed from:\n  - SYN_RENAME: WordNet synonym of each predicate's head lemma; keep camelCase; if no synonym, leave the name.\n  - REORDER: permute conjuncts.\n  - CONTRAPOS: ∀x(A→B) ↦ ∀x(¬B→¬A).\n  - DEMORGAN: push one ¬ through ∧/∨, or introduce ¬¬ + De Morgan.\n  - PRENEX.\n  Keep a rewrite only if it is solver-EQUIVALENT (under the rename map).\n1.10 Complexity features per sentence:\n  - n_tokens (spaCy);\n  - n_quant (gold quantifier count);\n  - depth (gold AST depth);\n  - n_cond = count of text markers {if, unless, except, only, either, neither, not, no, none, without, whether} + gold binary connective count (∧ ∨ → ↔ ⊕).\n  composite = mean of the 4 z-scores → tercile ∈ {low, mid, top} by 33/67 percentiles over the 300 sentences.\n1.11 Write data/screen_set.json:\n  - sentences [{sid, sha1, nl, story_id, gold_fol, gold_source, gold_fol_orig, features, tercile}];\n  - real [{item_id, sid, system, cand_fol, parse_ok, free_vars, declared_preds, label fields}];\n  - mutants [{mid, sid, operator, fol}];\n  - rewrites [{rid, sid, kind, fol, rename_map}];\n  - counts{}.\n  Also write data/screen_set_ids.txt (sorted real item_ids) for the Arm-B identity check.\n\n=== STEP 2. solver_sig.py (formula side; all CPU, parallel over formulas) ===\nsig_abs(F, N=3):\n  - For each predicate P: UP-violation = SAT(P⊆P' ∧ F(P) ∧ ¬F(P')); DOWN-violation = SAT(P⊆P' ∧ F(P') ∧ ¬F(P)), over sizes 1..N → label.\n  - For each constant c: the same test on the 'identity' predicate x=c. Optional; skip if slow.\n  - Role anchors: for each k-ary P (k≥2) and slot i, and for each unary A in F (and each constant c): anchored(P, i, A) iff F ↔ F[P ↦ λx̄. P(x̄) ∧ A(x_i)] is valid over sizes 1..N; anchored(P, i, c) likewise with x_i = c.\n  → S = {pred_labels, anchors}.\nsig_rel(F, N=3): for each ordered pair of unary predicates (P,Q), P≠Q, and side ∈ {in, out}: label of P when P ⊆ P' and P' differs from P only inside Q (or only outside ¬Q). Code from relativized_probe.py, with unique sort names. Constants: treat 'x=c' as a unary Q.\nUse z3 Solver with timeout 5 s per query. Unknown → coordinate '?' (excluded, counted as coverage loss).\nCompute sig_abs and sig_rel for: every gold (300), every parseable real candidate (~900), every mutant (≤3000) and every rewrite (≤600). Cache by the canonical string of the AST.\n\n=== STEP 3. Text side ===\n3.1 Concept extraction (text_sig.py, spaCy en_core_web_sm), a list of concepts with {cid, head_lemma, span, pos, role}:\n  - NOUN heads of noun chunks (skip pronouns and determiners-only);\n  - VERB tokens that are not AUX/copula;\n  - ADJ tokens (attributive and predicative);\n  - PROPN chunks become CONSTANTS (used for role anchoring and alignment coverage, not specialized).\n  Cap at 10 concepts per sentence, prioritising restrictor/scope content words.\n3.2 Specialized copies, deterministic templates:\n  - NOUN: insert a modifier adjective directly before the head (after existing determiners/adjectives); modifiers m1='European', m2='young' (skip one that already appears in the sentence).\n  - VERB: append ' on Mondays' (m1) / ' in Europe' (m2) at the right edge of the verb's subtree, excluding advcl/relcl/conj/ccomp children.\n  - ADJ: prefix 'very ' (m1) / 'European and ' (m2).\n  - Plural/singular agreement is kept by inserting before the head token only.\n  - Log each copy for manual spot-check.\n3.3 Relativized text probes (A2 only). Select concept pairs (c, q) where q is conj-coordinated with c, or q is in a relcl/amod/advcl ('unless', 'except', 'if') attached to c's head or to the same clause. At most 4 pairs per sentence. Copies: s[c → 'c that is/are (also) q-phrase'] (change only OUTSIDE q) and s[c → 'c that is/are not q-phrase'] (change only INSIDE q). Build them with the same template machinery; skip the pair if the grammar fails.\n3.4 LLM probe (A1/A2): ONE call per sentence (two if more than 40 questions) to google/gemini-2.5-flash, temperature 0, with the thinking config chosen in STEP 6.0.\n  Prompt: 'Treat each statement as a strict logical claim; ignore world knowledge beyond word meaning. For each numbered pair, answer whether statement A logically guarantees statement B (yes/no). Return JSON {\"1\":\"yes\",...}.'\n  Pairs per concept and modifier: (s, s_spec) = DOWN question; (s_spec, s) = UP question. The LLM NEVER sees a formula.\n  Label per concept per modifier: DOWN only → '−'; UP only → '+'; both → '0'; neither → '±'. Combine m1 and m2: agree → that label; disagree → '?' (inconsistent, excluded, counted).\n  Consistency flag: fraction of concepts with m1 == m2.\n  Role anchors (text): from the UD parse, for each verb/relational concept: slot0 ← nsubj concept (or constant), slot1 ← obj/obl concept (or constant). A passive nsubj:pass maps to slot1 and obl:agent to slot0.\n  → T = {concept_labels, anchors, rel_labels}.\n3.5 udep_marker.py (A3, zero-LLM):\n  - Clone eric11eca/Udep2Mono; apply the u75j patch (stanza package='gum'), stub pattern.en and install pqdict.\n  - Wrap it in try/except. On an obl:unmarked crash (unless) or any other exception, fall back to a compact rule-based marker over the stanza/spaCy parse:\n    - default ↑;\n    - flip for the scope of 'not/n't/no/never/none/without';\n    - the restrictor of every/all/each/no (the noun + its relcl/amod subtree) → ↓;\n    - the nuclear scope of 'no' → ↓;\n    - the antecedent of if/unless (advcl with mark if/unless) → ↓, then apply negation for unless: the unless-clause is ↑ under a negated consequent, following the Udep2Mono table;\n    - 'only X' → X ↑ and the scope ↓;\n    - generic bare plural or indefinite subject of a generic sentence ('A person is eligible if ...') → ↓.\n  - Concept label: ↑ → '+', ↓ → '−', '=' → '±'. No '0' is ever produced.\n  - Relativized coordinates from coordination: 'c and q' in a restrictor → P vacuous-outside-Q pattern; 'c or q' → P vacuous-inside-Q (per relativized_vacuity.py).\n  - Log which path produced each label (udep2mono vs rules).\n\n=== STEP 4. align.py (identical for A1, A2, A3 and B4/B5) ===\n- pred_tokens(P) = camelCase/underscore split → lowercase → WordNet lemma → drop stopwords.\n- concept_tokens(c) = lemmas of the head + its compound/amod modifiers.\n- overlap(P, c) = |pt ∩ ct| / |pt|.\n- Assign P → argmax_c overlap if ≥ 0.5. Otherwise use MiniLM (sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2) cosine between ' '.join(pt) and the concept span; assign if ≥ 0.5; else P is unaligned.\n- Constants are aligned to PROPN concepts by the same rule.\n- COMPOUND rule (pre-registered): if pt overlaps ≥2 concepts, P is compared ONLY with the concept matching its LAST token (the head); the other overlapped concepts count as covered.\n- Coverage = (# text concepts covered by ≥1 predicate) / (# text concepts).\n\n=== STEP 5. score.py → signature_score(T, S, align, variant) → {score, mismatches[], error_type_pred, covered} ===\nCoordinates:\n(i) Each text concept c with a label ≠ '?':\n  - no aligned predicate → mismatch 'dropped' (weight 1);\n  - else, for each aligned non-compound-other predicate P: mismatch if S.label[P] ≠ T.label[c] (weight 1 / |aligned P|).\n(ii) Each formula predicate that is unaligned, or has label '0' → mismatch 'added' (weight 1).\n(iii) Each text role slot (verb v, slot i, concept a): if the aligned relation R exists and the aligned predicate/constant for a exists, mismatch if NOT anchored(R, i, A) while anchored(R, 1-i, A) holds ('swap'); otherwise the slot counts as a match (weight 1).\n(iv) A2 only: each relativized text coordinate with a label ≠ '?' vs S.rel[(P_c, P_q, side)] (weight 0.5).\n(v) A3: coordinates (i)–(iii), plus the coordination-derived relativized coordinates (weight 0.5).\nscore = 1 − Σw·mismatch / Σw over the coordinates.\ncovered = parse_ok ∧ solver finished (at most 20% '?' coordinates) ∧ coverage ≥ 0.5 ∧ at least 1 text concept labelled. If not covered → score 0.5 (G1 rule); also store raw_score where it is computable.\nerror_type_pred, a pre-registered decision list evaluated in order:\n  1. any 'swap' → swapped_args;\n  2. a dropped concept AND a remaining predicate whose label changed to ± or mismatches → conflation;\n  3. a dropped concept → dropped_condition;\n  4. an added/vacuous predicate → added_condition;\n  5. a concept with aligned predicates of opposite sign → wrong_split;\n  6. both text-'−' (restrictor) and text-'+' (scope) concepts mismatch, with − ↦ + AND + ↦ − → reversed_implication;\n  7. restrictor concepts − ↦ + (or − ↦ ±) with scope unchanged → quantifier;\n  8. exactly one concept with + ↔ − → negation_polarity;\n  9. only relativized mismatches → connective_andor;\n  10. otherwise, if any mismatch → other; else none.\nPer-operator ground-truth type map: NEG→negation_polarity, QUANT→quantifier, IMPL_REV→reversed_implication, DROP→dropped_condition, ADD→added_condition, ARG_SWAP→swapped_args, MERGE→conflation, ANDOR→connective_andor, SCOPE→scope (predicted blind), CARD→cardinality (predicted blind).\nScore every real candidate, gold, mutant and rewrite for A1, A2 and A3 (the text signature is reused per sentence). Write rows to results/screen_scores.jsonl: {item_id, set ∈ real/gold/mutant/rewrite, metric, score, raw_score, error_type_pred, covered, coverage, n_coords, usd, seconds}. usd = that sentence's probe cost / number of items using it (amortised) — report both amortised and per-sentence cost.\n\n=== STEP 6. Baselines & controls (run_baselines.py; asyncio with a semaphore of 8, retries with backoff, per-call ledger) ===\n6.0 PROBE CONFIG PILOT, done FIRST and without screen labels: B6 on 100 MED items (50 up / 50 down), with gemini-2.5-flash thinking-off vs thinking budget 1024.\n  Pre-registered choice: use thinking-off unless its downward accuracy is < 0.80 AND thinking-on raises downward accuracy by ≥ 0.05.\n  The chosen config is used for the A1/A2 probe AND for B4. B1 uses thinking-off, fixed for the cross-arm anchor; record b1_config in the outputs.\nB1 (anchor): google/gemini-2.5-flash, temperature 0, EXACT prompt 'Sentence: {s}\\nFOL: {f}\\nGive the probability (0-100) that this FOL is a faithful formalization of the sentence. Reply with a number only.'\n  - Parse the first number; clip to 0–100, then /100.\n  - Non-number → one retry, then 0.5 with covered=False.\n  - Run on real (~900), gold (300), mutants (≤3000) and rewrites (≤600). Unparseable candidates are still judged (the raw string is sent).\nB1+ (tuned judge, OPTIONAL tier, run last if cum$ < 4.3): thinking budget 512, rubric prompt listing the 10 error types and asking to check each, then output 'SCORE: n'. Real candidates only. Reported as a sensitivity check; not used in G3.\nB2: parse_ok ∈ {0,1}. Also a solver-ok variant: parse_ok ∧ satisfiable.\nB3 round-trip, real candidates only. Prompt: 'Translate this first-order logic formula into one plain English sentence. Read predicate and constant names as words. Formula: {f}'.\n  - B3cos = MiniLM cosine(s, back).\n  - B3nli = min(P_entail(s⇒back), P_entail(back⇒s)) with MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli on GPU (CPU fallback: MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-base-mnli-fever-anli).\n  - Unparseable candidates are still sent as a string.\nB4 decomposed-judge ablation: same model and config as the probe.\n  Prompt shows the FOL and, for each predicate P, asks two questions: 'If P is replaced by a stricter predicate P′ (every P′ is P), must the formula stay true whenever it was true?' (→ down) and 'If P is replaced by a looser predicate P″ (every P is P″), ...' (→ up). For each binary predicate R and slot i with a unary A in F: 'Does the formula say anything about R-pairs whose i-th element is not A?' (→ anchoring).\n  → LLM_S (same structure as S) → scored with the SAME score.py against the SAME T.\n  Run on real (~900), gold (300), 40 mutants per operator (stratified seeded sample, ≤400) and 150 rewrites.\nB5 oracle ceiling: T_oracle(c) = S_gold.label[P_gold aligned to c]; concepts with no aligned gold predicate keep T's label. Anchors come from the gold's anchors mapped via alignment. Score = score.py(T_oracle, S_cand). Also B5-A2 with gold relativized coordinates.\nB6 text-oracle accuracy:\n  - 400 items with seed 0: MED.tsv (https://raw.githubusercontent.com/verypluming/MED/master/MED.tsv) 300, being 150 with 'upward_monotone' in genre and 150 with 'downward_monotone', balanced over gold_label; plus HELP output_en/pmb_train_v1.0.tsv 100 (50 up / 50 down if the columns allow — inspect the header; if HELP has no direction tag, take all 400 from MED).\n  - Probe-format prompt: batches of 20 pairs, same wording as 3.4.\n  - Report accuracy overall, by direction × gold_label, and balanced accuracy.\n  - Zero-LLM marker: on MED pairs whose sentence1 and sentence2 differ in exactly one contiguous token span (difflib), take the marker polarity of the replaced span in sentence1. Accuracy vs the genre direction tag (↑ for upward_monotone, ↓ for downward_monotone). Report n parsed and n usable.\nB7 structural (the user's pilot metrics, zero cost), per real candidate:\n  - arity_consistent = every atom's arity matches the story's declared 'Predicates:' list;\n  - n_undefined = predicates used but not declared;\n  - story_joint_sat = z3 SAT of the conjunction of ALL of that system's premises for the story (joint-load conflict = UNSAT; a story-level feature copied to each item).\n  - Composite B7 = arity_consistent ∧ n_undefined == 0 ∧ story_joint_sat.\nExtra in-domain diagnostic: agreement of T (LLM probe) and of the A3 marker with S_gold on aligned concepts of the 300 golds (concept-level accuracy, stratified by text label). This is a text-side accuracy estimate on FOLIO itself, contaminated by wrong gold, and it is reported as such.\n\n=== STEP 7. analyze.py ===\nPrimary label set L = real candidates with status ≠ unlabeled (also report the unlabeled count and rate).\nFor every metric M ∈ {A1, A2, A3, B1, B1+, B2, B3cos, B3nli, B4, B5, B5-A2, B7}:\n  - AUROC_real (sklearn roc_auc_score of score vs correct);\n  - 1000× sentence-clustered bootstrap (resample the 300 sids with replacement, take all their items) → 95% percentile CI;\n  - also AUROC under correct_orig if corrected gold exists;\n  - also a variant where uncovered-because-unparseable gets 0 instead of 0.5.\nPer tercile: AUROC and CI; Δ(M − B1) per tercile with a paired clustered bootstrap CI (same resamples); trend = Spearman(tercile index, Δ) and the slope of Δ across terciles with a bootstrap CI (the crossover test).\nG1 coverage = mean(covered) over ALL real candidates (unparseable included).\nG2 false alarms = fraction of rewrites with score(rewrite) < score(gold of same sentence) − δ, with δ = 0 for A1/A2/A3/B4/B5 and δ = 0.10 for continuous scores (report δ=0 too). Split A-variant false alarms by cause: alignment (rename rewrites) vs other.\nG3 increment: X0 = [B1, parse_ok]; X1 = X0 + [M]. LogisticRegression(C=1.0, standardised). Out-of-fold predictions via GroupKFold(5, groups=sid), repeated over 5 shuffles of group order (averaged). ΔAUROC = AUROC(oof1) − AUROC(oof0). 1000× clustered bootstrap on the OOF predictions → 95% CI; G3 passes iff the lower bound > 0. Also report the increment over [B1, parse_ok, B3nli, B7] (the best-baseline combination, a sensitivity check) and over [B1+, parse_ok] if B1+ ran.\nG4 = AUROC_real ≥ 0.65.\nMutants: per operator and metric, detection_strict = P(score(mut) < score(gold)); detection_tiehalf = P(<) + 0.5·P(=) (chance = 0.5). 95% Wilson CIs. The blind-spot test: A1 detection_tiehalf on SCOPE and CARD within [0.4, 0.6] and CI overlapping 0.5 = 'predicted blind spot confirmed'; also A1 on ANDOR is predicted blind, and A2 on ANDOR is predicted to detect.\n  Error-type confusion matrix (A1, A2, A3; rows = true operator type, cols = error_type_pred) on mutants with score < gold; macro recall over the 8 non-blind types.\nSystem level: per-system mean score vs per-system accuracy on L; Kendall τ (n=3, flagged uninterpretable).\nCost: $ per item (amortised and marginal) and seconds per item, per metric; total $ from the ledger.\nCorrelations between metric scores (Spearman), needed for the rule's combined-score clause.\nOptional, only if corrected gold was retrieved: (a) success criterion 5 is not computable without adjudication → deferred; (b) gold-error flagging = rank the 300 original golds by 1 − score(T, S_gold_orig) and report precision@50 vs base rate against the corrected==True flags.\n\n=== STEP 8. Selection rule, applied VERBATIM within this arm over the candidates A1, A2, A3 (B-rows are baselines/controls, not candidates) ===\n'Primary measure: AUROC_real, item-level AUROC of the metric score on the screen's real candidates against equivalence-up-to-renaming labels, with a 1000× bootstrap clustered by sentence. A candidate SURVIVES iff all four gates hold: (G1) Coverage: at least 70% of real candidates are scored. Unscored items get 0.5 and stay in. (G2) False alarms: at most 10% on solver-verified meaning-preserving rewrites. (G3) Increment: ΔAUROC of logistic(cheap_judge + parse_ok + metric) over logistic(cheap_judge + parse_ok) has a clustered-bootstrap 95% lower bound above 0. (G4) AUROC_real is at least 0.65. Ranking: survivors are ranked by AUROC_real in the TOP complexity tercile. A winner is declared only if it leads the next survivor there by at least 0.03. Otherwise the top two advance as a pre-specified combined score (logistic stack), provided their per-item score correlation is below 0.5; if it is not, the top one advances alone. If no candidate survives, the one with the largest ΔAUROC point estimate advances only if ΔAUROC ≥ 0.03. Otherwise the screen is reported as null, and iter 2 tests the full stack of all four against baselines on held-out. In iter 2 the rule is re-applied with the DATASET's audited screen labels; if the ranking changes, the audited ranking governs.'\nWrite verdict = {survivors, winner or pair or null, per-gate values, reason}.\nWrite the diagnosis block:\n  - B6 downward accuracy < 0.80 → 'text side is the bottleneck';\n  - B5 AUROC − A1 AUROC → the text-side gap;\n  - B4 vs A1 ΔAUROC with CI → 'exact semantics vs decomposition';\n  - median alignment coverage → the alignment bottleneck.\n\n=== STEP 9. Outputs ===\nresults/method_out.json (validate with the aii-json skill against the experiment output schema the executor is given). Contents:\n  - config (models, thinking configs, thresholds, seeds, gold_source counts);\n  - screen_set counts;\n  - per-metric table {AUROC_real, CI, per-tercile AUROC/CI, Δ vs B1 per tercile, G1–G4, false-alarm rate, cost/item, seconds/item};\n  - per-operator detection table;\n  - confusion matrices;\n  - B6 table;\n  - in-domain text-vs-gold agreement;\n  - label diagnostics (equiv rate per system, unlabeled rate, bounded-vs-unbounded agreement);\n  - the verdict;\n  - the diagnosis;\n  - examples[]: per-item rows {input: sentence, candidate_fol, output: label, predict_<metric>: score, metadata: item_id, system, tercile, error_type_pred}.\nAlso: data/screen_set.json, data/screen_set_ids.txt, results/screen_scores.jsonl, results/cost_ledger.jsonl, and figures (ROC per metric; Δ vs B1 by tercile; detection per operator heatmap) via aii-data-fig-gen.\nFinish with README.md and .aii/manifest.yaml. hf/stanza model caches and the Udep2Mono clone get delete: redownloadable with sources; data/ and results/ get keep.",
  "fallback_plan": "DATA:\n(1) If Logic-LM raw URLs fail, clone the repo (git clone --depth 1) or use the GitHub API contents endpoint.\n(2) If FOLIO v0.0 validation is missing, use the Yale-LILY/FOLIO git history, or HF yale-nlp/FOLIO / tasksource/folio, filtered to sentences that match the Logic-LM NL. Record the gold version.\n(3) If the corrected gold is unavailable (the likely case: 404 on 2026-09-23), use the original gold with gold_source='original' for all items, state it prominently, and mark success criteria 5 and 6 and the dual-label AUROC as deferred to iter 2 (DATASET L4 audit).\n(4) If fewer than 300 sentences match, use all matched sentences and report n. Do NOT loosen the matching normalisation, because Arm B must reproduce it. Report the fuzzy-match count (rapidfuzz ≥ 95) only as a diagnostic.\n\nLABELER:\n(5) If bijection search is too slow (projected more than 60 min on 7 cores after the 50-sentence stage), lower the domain bound to 1..3 for labels, log it, and keep the unbounded check for equiv items.\n(6) If more than 25% of real candidates end up 'unlabeled', report it and add a lexical-assist bijection as a SECONDARY label: try a name-similarity-ordered bijection first, still solver-checked. Primary AUROC stays on the blind label.\n\nFORMULA SIDE:\n(7) If sig_rel is too slow, restrict pairs to predicates co-occurring under the same quantifier and lower N to 2 for sig_rel only; log it.\n(8) Solver timeouts → '?' coordinates (coverage loss), never dropped silently.\n\nTEXT SIDE:\n(9) If template specialization produces ungrammatical copies for more than 20% of concepts (spot-check 30), switch those POS classes to the relative-clause template 'X that is European' / 'X, on Mondays,'. Do NOT let the LLM rewrite sentences.\n(10) If Udep2Mono cannot be installed within 30 min (Google Drive model, stanza version), use the compact rule-based marker only and name A3 'A3-rules'.\n(11) If gemini-2.5-flash is unavailable, or its pilot B6 downward accuracy is < 0.70 under both configs, switch the probe (and B4, for fairness) to openai/gpt-4.1-mini (8/10 in the feasibility probe) and record it. B1 stays gemini-2.5-flash for the cross-arm anchor.\n\nBUDGET (hard cap $5.80, checked before every batch):\n(12) Priority order: B6 pilot → A1/A2 probes → B1 on real + gold + rewrites → B1 on mutants → B4 on real + gold → B3 → B4 on mutants and rewrites → B1+.\n(13) If projected spend exceeds the cap, first shrink B1-on-mutants to 100 per operator, then B4 mutants to 20 per operator, then drop B1+.\n(14) If gemini returns reasoning tokens despite the thinking-off setting, re-estimate cost from the first 20 calls and apply the same cuts.\n\nANALYSIS:\n(15) If a tercile has fewer than 30 positives or 30 negatives, report its AUROC as 'underpowered' with the CI, and fall back to halves (top 50% vs bottom 50%) for the crossover trend. Log n_generated, n_equiv_dropped and n_NA per operator.\\n  Rewrites: 2 per gold, drawn by seed from:\\n  - SYN_RENAME: WordNet synonym of each predicate's head lemma; keep camelCase; if no synonym, leave the name.\\n  - REORDER: permute conjuncts.\\n  - CONTRAPOS: ∀x(A→B) ↦ ∀x(¬B→¬A).\\n  - DEMORGAN: push one ¬ through ∧/∨, or introduce ¬¬ + De Morgan.\\n  - PRENEX.\\n  Keep a rewrite only if it is solver-EQUIVALENT (under the rename map).\\n1.10 Complexity features per sentence:\\n  - n_tokens (spaCy);\\n  - n_quant (gold quantifier count);\\n  - depth (gold AST depth);\\n  - n_cond = count of text markers {if, unless, except, only, either, neither, not, no, none, without, whether} + gold binary connective count (∧ ∨ → ↔ ⊕).\\n  composite = mean of the 4 z-scores → tercile ∈ {low, mid, top} by 33/67 percentiles over the 300 sentences.\\n1.11 Write data/screen_set.json:\\n  - sentences [{sid, sha1, nl, story_id, gold_fol, gold_source, gold_fol_orig, features, tercile}];\\n  - real [{item_id, sid, system, cand_fol, parse_ok, free_vars, declared_preds, label fields}];\\n  - mutants [{mid, sid, operator, fol}];\\n  - rewrites [{rid, sid, kind, fol, rename_map}];\\n  - counts{}.\\n  Also write data/screen_set_ids.txt (sorted real item_ids) for the Arm-B identity check.\\n\\n=== STEP 2. solver_sig.py (formula side; all CPU, parallel over formulas) ===\\nsig_abs(F, N=3):\\n  - For each predicate P: UP-violation = SAT(P⊆P' ∧ F(P) ∧ ¬F(P')); DOWN-violation = SAT(P⊆P' ∧ F(P') ∧ ¬F(P)), over sizes 1..N → label.\\n  - For each constant c: the same test on the 'identity' predicate x=c. Optional; skip if slow.\\n  - Role anchors: for each k-ary P (k≥2) and slot i, and for each unary A in F (and each constant c): anchored(P, i, A) iff F ↔ F[P ↦ λx̄. P(x̄) ∧ A(x_i)] is valid over sizes 1..N; anchored(P, i, c) likewise with x_i = c.\\n  → S = {pred_labels, anchors}.\\nsig_rel(F, N=3): for each ordered pair of unary predicates (P,Q), P≠Q, and side ∈ {in, out}: label of P when P ⊆ P' and P' differs from P only inside Q (or only outside ¬Q). Code from relativized_probe.py, with unique sort names. Constants: treat 'x=c' as a unary Q.\\nUse z3 Solver with timeout 5 s per query. Unknown → coordinate '?' (excluded, counted as coverage loss).\\nCompute sig_abs and sig_rel for: every gold (300), every parseable real candidate (~900), every mutant (≤3000) and every rewrite (≤600). Cache by the canonical string of the AST.\\n\\n=== STEP 3. Text side ===\\n3.1 Concept extraction (text_sig.py, spaCy en_core_web_sm), a list of concepts with {cid, head_lemma, span, pos, role}:\\n  - NOUN heads of noun chunks (skip pronouns and determiners-only);\\n  - VERB tokens that are not AUX/copula;\\n  - ADJ tokens (attributive and predicative);\\n  - PROPN chunks become CONSTANTS (used for role anchoring and alignment coverage, not specialized).\\n  Cap at 10 concepts per sentence, prioritising restrictor/scope content words.\\n3.2 Specialized copies, deterministic templates:\\n  - NOUN: insert a modifier adjective directly before the head (after existing determiners/adjectives); modifiers m1='European', m2='young' (skip one that already appears in the sentence).\\n  - VERB: append ' on Mondays' (m1) / ' in Europe' (m2) at the right edge of the verb's subtree, excluding advcl/relcl/conj/ccomp children.\\n  - ADJ: prefix 'very ' (m1) / 'European and ' (m2).\\n  - Plural/singular agreement is kept by inserting before the head token only.\\n  - Log each copy for manual spot-check.\\n3.3 Relativized text probes (A2 only). Select concept pairs (c, q) where q is conj-coordinated with c, or q is in a relcl/amod/advcl ('unless', 'except', 'if') attached to c's head or to the same clause. At most 4 pairs per sentence. Copies: s[c → 'c that is/are (also) q-phrase'] (change only OUTSIDE q) and s[c → 'c that is/are not q-phrase'] (change only INSIDE q). Build them with the same template machinery; skip the pair if the grammar fails.\\n3.4 LLM probe (A1/A2): ONE call per sentence (two if more than 40 questions) to google/gemini-2.5-flash, temperature 0, with the thinking config chosen in STEP 6.0.\\n  Prompt: 'Treat each statement as a strict logical claim; ignore world knowledge beyond word meaning. For each numbered pair, answer whether statement A logically guarantees statement B (yes/no). Return JSON {\\\"1\\\":\\\"yes\\\",...}.'\\n  Pairs per concept and modifier: (s, s_spec) = DOWN question; (s_spec, s) = UP question. The LLM NEVER sees a formula.\\n  Label per concept per modifier: DOWN only → '−'; UP only → '+'; both → '0'; neither → '±'. Combine m1 and m2: agree → that label; disagree → '?' (inconsistent, excluded, counted).\\n  Consistency flag: fraction of concepts with m1 == m2.\\n  Role anchors (text): from the UD parse, for each verb/relational concept: slot0 ← nsubj concept (or constant), slot1 ← obj/obl concept (or constant). A passive nsubj:pass maps to slot1 and obl:agent to slot0.\\n  → T = {concept_labels, anchors, rel_labels}.\\n3.5 udep_marker.py (A3, zero-LLM):\\n  - Clone eric11eca/Udep2Mono; apply the u75j patch (stanza package='gum'), stub pattern.en and install pqdict.\\n  - Wrap it in try/except. On an obl:unmarked crash (unless) or any other exception, fall back to a compact rule-based marker over the stanza/spaCy parse:\\n    - default ↑;\\n    - flip for the scope of 'not/n't/no/never/none/without';\\n    - the restrictor of every/all/each/no (the noun + its relcl/amod subtree) → ↓;\\n    - the nuclear scope of 'no' → ↓;\\n    - the antecedent of if/unless (advcl with mark if/unless) → ↓, then apply negation for unless: the unless-clause is ↑ under a negated consequent, following the Udep2Mono table;\\n    - 'only X' → X ↑ and the scope ↓;\\n    - generic bare plural or indefinite subject of a generic sentence ('A person is eligible if ...') → ↓.\\n  - Concept label: ↑ → '+', ↓ → '−', '=' → '±'. No '0' is ever produced.\\n  - Relativized coordinates from coordination: 'c and q' in a restrictor → P vacuous-outside-Q pattern; 'c or q' → P vacuous-inside-Q (per relativized_vacuity.py).\\n  - Log which path produced each label (udep2mono vs rules).\\n\\n=== STEP 4. align.py (identical for A1, A2, A3 and B4/B5) ===\\n- pred_tokens(P) = camelCase/underscore split → lowercase → WordNet lemma → drop stopwords.\\n- concept_tokens(c) = lemmas of the head + its compound/amod modifiers.\\n- overlap(P, c) = |pt ∩ ct| / |pt|.\\n- Assign P → argmax_c overlap if ≥ 0.5. Otherwise use MiniLM (sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2) cosine between ' '.join(pt) and the concept span; assign if ≥ 0.5; else P is unaligned.\\n- Constants are aligned to PROPN concepts by the same rule.\\n- COMPOUND rule (pre-registered): if pt overlaps ≥2 concepts, P is compared ONLY with the concept matching its LAST token (the head); the other overlapped concepts count as covered.\\n- Coverage = (# text concepts covered by ≥1 predicate) / (# text concepts).\\n\\n=== STEP 5. score.py → signature_score(T, S, align, variant) → {score, mismatches[], error_type_pred, covered} ===\\nCoordinates:\\n(i) Each text concept c with a label ≠ '?':\\n  - no aligned predicate → mismatch 'dropped' (weight 1);\\n  - else, for each aligned non-compound-other predicate P: mismatch if S.label[P] ≠ T.label[c] (weight 1 / |aligned P|).\\n(ii) Each formula predicate that is unaligned, or has label '0' → mismatch 'added' (weight 1).\\n(iii) Each text role slot (verb v, slot i, concept a): if the aligned relation R exists and the aligned predicate/constant for a exists, mismatch if NOT anchored(R, i, A) while anchored(R, 1-i, A) holds ('swap'); otherwise the slot counts as a match (weight 1).\\n(iv) A2 only: each relativized text coordinate with a label ≠ '?' vs S.rel[(P_c, P_q, side)] (weight 0.5).\\n(v) A3: coordinates (i)–(iii), plus the coordination-derived relativized coordinates (weight 0.5).\\nscore = 1 − Σw·mismatch / Σw over the coordinates.\\ncovered = parse_ok ∧ solver finished (at most 20% '?' coordinates) ∧ coverage ≥ 0.5 ∧ at least 1 text concept labelled. If not covered → score 0.5 (G1 rule); also store raw_score where it is computable.\\nerror_type_pred, a pre-registered decision list evaluated in order:\\n  1. any 'swap' → swapped_args;\\n  2. a dropped concept AND a remaining predicate whose label changed to ± or mismatches → conflation;\\n  3. a dropped concept → dropped_condition;\\n  4. an added/vacuous predicate → added_condition;\\n  5. a concept with aligned predicates of opposite sign → wrong_split;\\n  6. both text-'−' (restrictor) and text-'+' (scope) concepts mismatch, with − ↦ + AND + ↦ − → reversed_implication;\\n  7. restrictor concepts − ↦ + (or − ↦ ±) with scope unchanged → quantifier;\\n  8. exactly one concept with + ↔ − → negation_polarity;\\n  9. only relativized mismatches → connective_andor;\\n  10. otherwise, if any mismatch → other; else none.\\nPer-operator ground-truth type map: NEG→negation_polarity, QUANT→quantifier, IMPL_REV→reversed_implication, DROP→dropped_condition, ADD→added_condition, ARG_SWAP→swapped_args, MERGE→conflation, ANDOR→connective_andor, SCOPE→scope (predicted blind), CARD→cardinality (predicted blind).\\nScore every real candidate, gold, mutant and rewrite for A1, A2 and A3 (the text signature is reused per sentence). Write rows to results/screen_scores.jsonl: {item_id, set ∈ real/gold/mutant/rewrite, metric, score, raw_score, error_type_pred, covered, coverage, n_coords, usd, seconds}. usd = that sentence's probe cost / number of items using it (amortised) — report both amortised and per-sentence cost.\\n\\n=== STEP 6. Baselines & controls (run_baselines.py; asyncio with a semaphore of 8, retries with backoff, per-call ledger) ===\\n6.0 PROBE CONFIG PILOT, done FIRST and without screen labels: B6 on 100 MED items (50 up / 50 down), with gemini-2.5-flash thinking-off vs thinking budget 1024.\\n  Pre-registered choice: use thinking-off unless its downward accuracy is < 0.80 AND thinking-on raises downward accuracy by ≥ 0.05.\\n  The chosen config is used for the A1/A2 probe AND for B4. B1 uses thinking-off, fixed for the cross-arm anchor; record b1_config in the outputs.\\nB1 (anchor): google/gemini-2.5-flash, temperature 0, EXACT prompt 'Sentence: {s}\\\\nFOL: {f}\\\\nGive the probability (0-100) that this FOL is a faithful formalization of the sentence. Reply with a number only.'\\n  - Parse the first number; clip to 0–100, then /100.\\n  - Non-number → one retry, then 0.5 with covered=False.\\n  - Run on real (~900), gold (300), mutants (≤3000) and rewrites (≤600). Unparseable candidates are still judged (the raw string is sent).\\nB1+ (tuned judge, OPTIONAL tier, run last if cum$ < 4.3): thinking budget 512, rubric prompt listing the 10 error types and asking to check each, then output 'SCORE: n'. Real candidates only. Reported as a sensitivity check; not used in G3.\\nB2: parse_ok ∈ {0,1}. Also a solver-ok variant: parse_ok ∧ satisfiable.\\nB3 round-trip, real candidates only. Prompt: 'Translate this first-order logic formula into one plain English sentence. Read predicate and constant names as words. Formula: {f}'.\\n  - B3cos = MiniLM cosine(s, back).\\n  - B3nli = min(P_entail(s⇒back), P_entail(back⇒s)) with MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli on GPU (CPU fallback: MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-base-mnli-fever-anli).\\n  - Unparseable candidates are still sent as a string.\\nB4 decomposed-judge ablation: same model and config as the probe.\\n  Prompt shows the FOL and, for each predicate P, asks two questions: 'If P is replaced by a stricter predicate P′ (every P′ is P), must the formula stay true whenever it was true?' (→ down) and 'If P is replaced by a looser predicate P″ (every P is P″), ...' (→ up). For each binary predicate R and slot i with a unary A in F: 'Does the formula say anything about R-pairs whose i-th element is not A?' (→ anchoring).\\n  → LLM_S (same structure as S) → scored with the SAME score.py against the SAME T.\\n  Run on real (~900), gold (300), 40 mutants per operator (stratified seeded sample, ≤400) and 150 rewrites.\\nB5 oracle ceiling: T_oracle(c) = S_gold.label[P_gold aligned to c]; concepts with no aligned gold predicate keep T's label. Anchors come from the gold's anchors mapped via alignment. Score = score.py(T_oracle, S_cand). Also B5-A2 with gold relativized coordinates.\\nB6 text-oracle accuracy:\\n  - 400 items with seed 0: MED.tsv (https://raw.githubusercontent.com/verypluming/MED/master/MED.tsv) 300, being 150 with 'upward_monotone' in genre and 150 with 'downward_monotone', balanced over gold_label; plus HELP output_en/pmb_train_v1.0.tsv 100 (50 up / 50 down if the columns allow — inspect the header; if HELP has no direction tag, take all 400 from MED).\\n  - Probe-format prompt: batches of 20 pairs, same wording as 3.4.\\n  - Report accuracy overall, by direction × gold_label, and balanced accuracy.\\n  - Zero-LLM marker: on MED pairs whose sentence1 and sentence2 differ in exactly one contiguous token span (difflib), take the marker polarity of the replaced span in sentence1. Accuracy vs the genre direction tag (↑ for upward_monotone, ↓ for downward_monotone). Report n parsed and n usable.\\nB7 structural (the user's pilot metrics, zero cost), per real candidate:\\n  - arity_consistent = every atom's arity matches the story's declared 'Predicates:' list;\\n  - n_undefined = predicates used but not declared;\\n  - story_joint_sat = z3 SAT of the conjunction of ALL of that system's premises for the story (joint-load conflict = UNSAT; a story-level feature copied to each item).\\n  - Composite B7 = arity_consistent ∧ n_undefined == 0 ∧ story_joint_sat.\\nExtra in-domain diagnostic: agreement of T (LLM probe) and of the A3 marker with S_gold on aligned concepts of the 300 golds (concept-level accuracy, stratified by text label). This is a text-side accuracy estimate on FOLIO itself, contaminated by wrong gold, and it is reported as such.\\n\\n=== STEP 7. analyze.py ===\\nPrimary label set L = real candidates with status ≠ unlabeled (also report the unlabeled count and rate).\\nFor every metric M ∈ {A1, A2, A3, B1, B1+, B2, B3cos, B3nli, B4, B5, B5-A2, B7}:\\n  - AUROC_real (sklearn roc_auc_score of score vs correct);\\n  - 1000× sentence-clustered bootstrap (resample the 300 sids with replacement, take all their items) → 95% percentile CI;\\n  - also AUROC under correct_orig if corrected gold exists;\\n  - also a variant where uncovered-because-unparseable gets 0 instead of 0.5.\\nPer tercile: AUROC and CI; Δ(M − B1) per tercile with a paired clustered bootstrap CI (same resamples); trend = Spearman(tercile index, Δ) and the slope of Δ across terciles with a bootstrap CI (the crossover test).\\nG1 coverage = mean(covered) over ALL real candidates (unparseable included).\\nG2 false alarms = fraction of rewrites with score(rewrite) < score(gold of same sentence) − δ, with δ = 0 for A1/A2/A3/B4/B5 and δ = 0.10 for continuous scores (report δ=0 too). Split A-variant false alarms by cause: alignment (rename rewrites) vs other.\\nG3 increment: X0 = [B1, parse_ok]; X1 = X0 + [M]. LogisticRegression(C=1.0, standardised). Out-of-fold predictions via GroupKFold(5, groups=sid), repeated over 5 shuffles of group order (averaged). ΔAUROC = AUROC(oof1) − AUROC(oof0). 1000× clustered bootstrap on the OOF predictions → 95% CI; G3 passes iff the lower bound > 0. Also report the increment over [B1, parse_ok, B3nli, B7] (the best-baseline combination, a sensitivity check) and over [B1+, parse_ok] if B1+ ran.\\nG4 = AUROC_real ≥ 0.65.\\nMutants: per operator and metric, detection_strict = P(score(mut) < score(gold)); detection_tiehalf = P(<) + 0.5·P(=) (chance = 0.5). 95% Wilson CIs. The blind-spot test: A1 detection_tiehalf on SCOPE and CARD within [0.4, 0.6] and CI overlapping 0.5 = 'predicted blind spot confirmed'; also A1 on ANDOR is predicted blind, and A2 on ANDOR is predicted to detect.\\n  Error-type confusion matrix (A1, A2, A3; rows = true operator type, cols = error_type_pred) on mutants with score < gold; macro recall over the 8 non-blind types.\\nSystem level: per-system mean score vs per-system accuracy on L; Kendall τ (n=3, flagged uninterpretable).\\nCost: $ per item (amortised and marginal) and seconds per item, per metric; total $ from the ledger.\\nCorrelations between metric scores (Spearman), needed for the rule's combined-score clause.\\nOptional, only if corrected gold was retrieved: (a) success criterion 5 is not computable without adjudication → deferred; (b) gold-error flagging = rank the 300 original golds by 1 − score(T, S_gold_orig) and report precision@50 vs base rate against the corrected==True flags.\\n\\n=== STEP 8. Selection rule, applied VERBATIM within this arm over the candidates A1, A2, A3 (B-rows are baselines/controls, not candidates) ===\\n'Primary measure: AUROC_real, item-level AUROC of the metric score on the screen's real candidates against equivalence-up-to-renaming labels, with a 1000× bootstrap clustered by sentence. A candidate SURVIVES iff all four gates hold: (G1) Coverage: at least 70% of real candidates are scored. Unscored items get 0.5 and stay in. (G2) False alarms: at most 10% on solver-verified meaning-preserving rewrites. (G3) Increment: ΔAUROC of logistic(cheap_judge + parse_ok + metric) over logistic(cheap_judge + parse_ok) has a clustered-bootstrap 95% lower bound above 0. (G4) AUROC_real is at least 0.65. Ranking: survivors are ranked by AUROC_real in the TOP complexity tercile. A winner is declared only if it leads the next survivor there by at least 0.03. Otherwise the top two advance as a pre-specified combined score (logistic stack), provided their per-item score correlation is below 0.5; if it is not, the top one advances alone. If no candidate survives, the one with the largest ΔAUROC point estimate advances only if ΔAUROC ≥ 0.03. Otherwise the screen is reported as null, and iter 2 tests the full stack of all four against baselines on held-out. In iter 2 the rule is re-applied with the DATASET's audited screen labels; if the ranking changes, the audited ranking governs.'\\nWrite verdict = {survivors, winner or pair or null, per-gate values, reason}.\\nWrite the diagnosis block:\\n  - B6 downward accuracy < 0.80 → 'text side is the bottleneck';\\n  - B5 AUROC − A1 AUROC → the text-side gap;\\n  - B4 vs A1 ΔAUROC with CI → 'exact semantics vs decomposition';\\n  - median alignment coverage → the alignment bottleneck.\\n\\n=== STEP 9. Outputs ===\\nresults/method_out.json (validate with the aii-json skill against the experiment output schema the executor is given). Contents:\\n  - config (models, thinking configs, thresholds, seeds, gold_source counts);\\n  - screen_set counts;\\n  - per-metric table {AUROC_real, CI, per-tercile AUROC/CI, Δ vs B1 per tercile, G1–G4, false-alarm rate, cost/item, seconds/item};\\n  - per-operator detection table;\\n  - confusion matrices;\\n  - B6 table;\\n  - in-domain text-vs-gold agreement;\\n  - label diagnostics (equiv rate per system, unlabeled rate, bounded-vs-unbounded agreement);\\n  - the verdict;\\n  - the diagnosis;\\n  - examples[]: per-item rows {input: sentence, candidate_fol, output: label, predict_<metric>: score, metadata: item_id, system, tercile, error_type_pred}.\\nAlso: data/screen_set.json, data/screen_set_ids.txt, results/screen_scores.jsonl, results/cost_ledger.jsonl, and figures (ROC per metric; Δ vs B1 by tercile; detection per operator heatmap) via aii-data-fig-gen.\\nFinish with README.md and .aii/manifest.yaml. hf/stanza model caches and the Udep2Mono clone get delete: redownloadable with sources; data/ and results/ get keep.\",\n  \"fallback_plan\": \"DATA:\\n(1) If Logic-LM raw URLs fail, clone the repo (git clone --depth 1) or use the GitHub API contents endpoint.\\n(2) If FOLIO v0.0 validation is missing, use the Yale-LILY/FOLIO git history, or HF yale-nlp/FOLIO / tasksource/folio, filtered to sentences that match the Logic-LM NL. Record the gold version.\\n(3) If the corrected gold is unavailable (the likely case: 404 on 2026-09-23), use the original gold with gold_source='original' for all items, state it prominently, and mark success criteria 5 and 6 and the dual-label AUROC as deferred to iter 2 (DATASET L4 audit).\\n(4) If fewer than 300 sentences match, use all matched sentences and report n. Do NOT loosen the matching normalisation, because Arm B must reproduce it. Report the fuzzy-match count (rapidfuzz ≥ 95) only as a diagnostic.\\n\\nLABELER:\\n(5) If bijection search is too slow (projected more than 60 min on 7 cores after the 50-sentence stage), lower the domain bound to 1..3 for labels, log it, and keep the unbounded check for equiv items.\\n(6) If more than 25% of real candidates end up 'unlabeled', report it and add a lexical-assist bijection as a SECONDARY label: try a name-similarity-ordered bijection first, still solver-checked. Primary AUROC stays on the blind label.\\n\\nFORMULA SIDE:\\n(7) If sig_rel is too slow, restrict pairs to predicates co-occurring under the same quantifier and lower N to 2 for sig_rel only; log it.\\n(8) Solver timeouts → '?' coordinates (coverage loss), never dropped silently.\\n\\nTEXT SIDE:\\n(9) If template specialization produces ungrammatical copies for more than 20% of concepts (spot-check 30), switch those POS classes to the relative-clause template 'X that is European' / 'X, on Mondays,'. Do NOT let the LLM rewrite sentences.\\n(10) If Udep2Mono cannot be installed within 30 min (Google Drive model, stanza version), use the compact rule-based marker only and name A3 'A3-rules'.\\n(11) If gemini-2.5-flash is unavailable, or its pilot B6 downward accuracy is < 0.70 under both configs, switch the probe (and B4, for fairness) to openai/gpt-4.1-mini (8/10 in the feasibility probe) and record it. B1 stays gemini-2.5-flash for the cross-arm anchor.\\n\\nBUDGET (hard cap $5.80, checked before every batch):\\n(12) Priority order: B6 pilot → A1/A2 probes → B1 on real + gold + rewrites → B1 on mutants → B4 on real + gold → B3 → B4 on mutants and rewrites → B1+.\\n(13) If projected spend exceeds the cap, first shrink B1-on-mutants to 100 per operator, then B4 mutants to 20 per operator, then drop B1+.\\n(14) If gemini returns reasoning tokens despite the thinking-off setting, re-estimate cost from the first 20 calls and apply the same cuts.\\n\\nANALYSIS:\\n(15) If a tercile has fewer than 30 positives or 30 negatives, report its AUROC as 'underpowered' with the CI, and fall back to halves (top 50% vs bottom 50%) for the crossover trend. The rule's top-tercile ranking is still applied as written.\\n(16) If the label class balance is extreme (more than 85% one class), report PR-AUC alongside AUROC.\\n(17) If every A-variant fails G1 because of alignment coverage, report the coverage distribution and the B5 oracle, and run an extra diagnostic A1-oracle-align, which uses the labeler's bijection where one exists, to show how much of the failure is alignment. It is clearly marked as NOT gold-free and NOT a candidate.\",\n  \"testing_plan\": \"Run in stages; each stage gates the next.\\n\\nT0 UNIT (no API, under 10 min):\\n(a) Parser round-trips all 12 gen_hypo test formulas and 50 random FOLIO gold lines. The parse rate on FOLIO gold should be ≥ 95%; inspect failures.\\n(b) sig_abs reproduces the prototype table. All dogs that are not trained bark: Dog −, Trained +, Bark +. The contrapositive gives an identical signature. The vacuous (Loud ∨ ¬Loud) gives Loud 0. Xor gives ±. The 'Student reads some book' arg swap changes anchors.\\n(c) sig_rel reproduces the review probe: ∧↔∨ and swapped consequents give ≥ 6 differing coordinates; the contrapositive gives 0.\\n(d) The labeler says EQUIV for gold vs its own predicate-renamed copy, its contrapositive and its De Morgan rewrite, and NON-EQUIV for each hand mutant. Profiling the same formula twice does not crash (the unique-sort fix).\\n(e) The Udep2Mono / rules marker marks 'All dogs that are not trained bark' as dogs ↓, trained ↑, bark ↑, and does not crash on an 'unless' sentence.\\n(f) Aligner: UntrainedDog aligns to dog (head) and covers 'trained'; a WordNet-renamed predicate (Bark → Yap) aligns via MiniLM or is recorded as unaligned.\\n\\nT1 MINI (10 sentences, about 30 real candidates, $<0.10):\\n- screen set build end-to-end; print matched counts;\\n- one probe call per sentence: check the JSON parse rate is 100% and reasoning_tokens == 0 under thinking-off;\\n- the ledger records usage.cost;\\n- hand-inspect the specialized copies for all 10 sentences, the text labels, the alignment table and the per-candidate mismatch lists.\\nAcceptance:\\n- the gold's own A1 score is 1.0 on at least 6/10 sentences (the text-vs-gold agreement sanity check);\\n- rewrites of gold score equal to gold on at least 90%;\\n- mutants of NEG/IMPL_REV/DROP score below gold on most items.\\nIf the gold full-match rate is below 50%, debug the text side or the aligner BEFORE scaling. Use only gold, mutants and rewrites for this check, never real-candidate labels, to avoid tuning on the screen.\\n\\nT2 B6 PILOT (100 MED items, both thinking configs, about $0.10): apply the pre-registered config choice; check that downward accuracy is reported separately.\\n\\nT3 SCALE 50 sentences: time every stage and extrapolate to 300 (aii-long-running-tasks). Check solver timeouts (< 5%), labeler unlabeled rate (< 25%), spend per sentence against the budget table (probe ≈ $0.003/sentence; B1 ≈ $0.0001/call; B4 ≈ $0.001/call), and the equiv rate per system. Expected ordering: gpt-4 > gpt-3.5 ≈ davinci; a reversed order signals a labeler bug.\\n\\nT4 FULL 300 sentences. Keep threshold constants frozen after T1: they are set only from gold/mutant/rewrite behaviour, never from real-candidate AUROC.\\n\\nSANITY CHECKS BEFORE WRITING THE VERDICT:\\n- B5 oracle AUROC ≥ A1 AUROC. Handle both list and newline-joined string fields.\\n1.4 norm(s) = re.sub(r'\\\\s+', ' ', s.lower()).strip().rstrip('.'). Match Logic-LM NL lines to gold NL by norm equality. Log n_lines, n_matched and n_unmatched per system; unmatched lines are excluded.\\n1.5 Gold per sentence: corrected if available (gold_source='corrected'), else original.
