description: run_qY2a2IS-WLIs iter1 GEN_PLAN experiment_1 (Screen Arm A) — signature A1/A2/A3 plan; data facts (Logic-LM file format, MED/HELP files, corrected-gold fallbacks), budget and pre-registered scoring rules

Plan for Screen Arm A (2026-09-23). Follows [[project-nl2fol-faithfulness-genstrat-iter1-run-qy2a2is-wlis]].

DATA FACTS VERIFIED
- Logic-LM FOLIO_dev_*.json entries have the keys {id, context, question, answer, options, raw_logic_programs}. raw_logic_programs[0] holds Predicates/Premises/Conclusion blocks of "FOL ::: NL" lines and sometimes uses ∈.
- FOLIO v0.0 lives at data/v0.0/folio-validation.jsonl.
- MED.tsv: gold_label ∈ {entailment, neutral}; genre carries upward_monotone / downward_monotone.
- HELP is output_en/pmb_train_v1.0.tsv, a TRAIN set.
- The 2606.02837 repo dslab-uniud/Fixing-FOLIO-and-MALLS is still 404. Its fields are FOL_sentence_old / FOL_sentence_new / corrected.
- Fallback for corrected gold: HF yfxiao/folio-refined (from [[project-nl2fol-faithfulness-reviewhypo-run-u75jrhuss0zo]]), or the arXiv e-print tarball.

PLAN CHOICES
- Budget: hard cap $5.8, mandatory spend about $3.7.
- The B4 decomposed judge is the biggest cost, about $2.
- The probe's thinking config is chosen on a MED pilot, never on screen labels.
- Pre-registered alignment rules: compare a compound predicate only against its head concept; flip the sign for lexical negation.
- The score is a mismatch FRACTION, because per-concept errors compound.
- B7 structural metrics come free from Logic-LM's declared predicate lists.
- G3 uses out-of-fold GroupKFold predictions by sentence, not in-sample logistic.

### [84] ASSISTANT · 2026-09-23 12:02:53 UTC

```
The plan for Screen Arm A is written to `./.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json`. I couldn't run a JSON parser here, so I only checked the file by eye and with pattern searches for stray quotes and escapes. It should be validated before the executor picks it up.

**What the plan does:**
- **Builds the shared screen set** from the strategy's exact recipe, so Arm B gets the same item ids. That means Logic-LM's released FOLIO-dev outputs from 3 systems, the first 300 matched sentences sorted by hash (about 900 real candidates), and 10 kinds of typed mutants plus 2 meaning-preserving rewrites per gold formula.
  - Each candidate is labelled correct or wrong by a z3 check for equivalence to gold under some renaming of predicates and constants.
  - The labeler never looks at names, so it stays independent of the metric's word-overlap aligner.
- **Scores three signature variants:** absolute (A1), relativized (A2) and a version with no LLM on the text side (A3). Scores are mismatch fractions rather than a yes/no flag, because per-concept errors compound. Error types come from a fixed decision list.
- **Runs the baselines and controls on the same items:**
  - B1 is the fixed-prompt gemini judge, shared with Arm B as the anchor; B1+ is an optional tuned version.
  - B2 is the parse rate, B3 is round-trip (embedding similarity and NLI).
  - B4 is the per-concept LLM judge (does the gain come from the solver or just from asking per concept?).
  - B5 is the ceiling if the text side were perfect; B6 checks the text probe's accuracy on MED/HELP, split upward/downward.
  - B7 is the user's structural metrics. These cost nothing here because Logic-LM declares its predicates for each story.
- **Analysis:** AUROC with confidence intervals resampled by sentence, the four gates (G3 uses out-of-fold predictions), results per complexity tercile, detection per mutant type, and a check that scope and cardinality errors really are invisible to the signature, as predicted. The selection rule is copied word for word from the strategy.
- **Budget:** hard cap of $5.80, with the required steps estimated at about $3.70. Whether the probe model "thinks" is decided on a 100-item MED pilot, never on the screen labels.

**What it reuses:** the run's z3 prototypes and the reviewer's relativized and blind-spot probes. It also uses a sibling run's Udep2Mono patch and that run's review findings. From those it adopts:
- the `yfxiao/folio-refined` corrected-gold fallback;
- an alignment rule for words like "unemployed" written as ¬Employed;
- a caveat that FOLIO has almost no exception clauses, so the predicted advantage on long, conditioned sentences is weakly tested here.

**Main limitations:**
- The corrected gold from arXiv:2606.02837 is still 404. The labels will likely rest on original or refined gold, so the checks that need corrected labels (full-match rate on correct-but-inequivalent candidates, precision@50 for flagging wrong gold) are deferred to iteration 2.
- Kendall τ over only 3 systems is flagged as uninterpretable.

I also wrote a README and an empty `.aii/manifest.yaml` (no heavy files), and saved a memory note for this step.
```
